# VSS2 capture library

Generated by `stagecraft library` (PLANNED_WORK.md §7, E3). One row per distinct sound
(settings differing only in name, colour, pin id or preset path are one row). Measured on a
mono source (same signal into both inputs):

- **Mic taps**: VSS2 simulates its main microphones: each output gets the direct sound
  from its own capsule and from the centre capsule (Decca tree). Per output: capsule, ms after
  VSS2's 4 ms latency, peak level in dB. **Fit**: largest difference between measured and
  geometric (speed of sound) arrival, ms.
- **Level L / R**: direct sound (all its taps), 1 kHz octave, dB.
- **Tone**: direct sound at 4 / 8 / 16 kHz relative to 1 kHz (air absorption; a point source
  with no air setting is ≈ 0).
- **Early / late**: reflection energy up to 80 ms / after 80 ms, relative to the direct sound
  (1 kHz octave).
- **Reflections**: the strongest early reflections, ms after the direct sound and dB
  relative to its peak.
